[av_codeblock wrapper_element='' wrapper_element_attributes='' codeblock_type='' deactivate_wrapper='aviaTBdeactivate_wrapper' alb_description='' id='' custom_class='' template_class='' av_uid='av-mmtxzha0' sc_version='1.0']
<style>
strong.gradient.hero-gradient {
	font-weight: 900!important; font-size: 130%;
	display: flex;
	margin-block: 10px;
	line-height: 1.2;
	filter:  contrast(1.16) brightness(1.2);
} 
.html_header_sidebar.html_content_align_left .container {
	max-width: unset;
	float: none;
	margin: 0px auto;
}
.av-section-color-overlay {
	    background: linear-gradient(to right, var(--altcolor), var(--maincolor))!important;
mix-blend-mode: soft-light;
	opacity: 1!important;
}
.caption_left .slideshow_caption {
	left: auto;
	right: 0;
	background: #000000a8;
	backdrop-filter: blur(7px) grayscale(0.13);
	transition: all 600ms ease;
}
.caption_left .slideshow_caption:hover {
	left: auto;
	right: 0;
	background: #000000a8;
	backdrop-filter: blur(0) grayscale(0);
}
.caption_left .slideshow_caption *{
	  filter: drop-shadow(0px 0px 4px #ffffff11) drop-shadow(0px 0px 14px #ffffff11);

}
/* ─── §5c  Grain / Noise Overlay ────────────────────────────────────── */

/* Grain SVG — inline, no external dependency */
:root {
  --ts-grain: url("data:image/svg+xml,<svg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' width='400' height='400'><filter id='g'><feTurbulence type='fractalNoise' baseFrequency='0.65' numOctaves='4' stitchTiles='stitch'/><feColorMatrix type='saturate' values='0'/></filter><rect width='400' height='400' filter='url(%23g)'/></svg>");
}

.ts-grain {
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.ts-grain::before {
	content: '';
	position: absolute;
	inset: -20%;
	width: 140%;
	height: 140%;
	background-image: var(--ts-grain);
	background-size: 200px 200px;
	background-repeat: repeat;
	opacity: 0.5;
	mix-blend-mode: soft-light;
	pointer-events: none;
	z-index: 1;
	will-change: opacity;
}
.ts-grain:not(.ts-grain-animated, .ts-grain-flickered, .ts-grain-perf)::before {
	background-attachment: fixed;
}
/* When combined with grid-bg, tone down noise so dots/grid remain visible.
   Opacity driven by JS config.noiseOpacity → --ts-grid-noise-opacity */
.grid-bg.ts-grain::before {
  opacity: var(--ts-grid-noise-opacity, 0.7);
}

/* Animated grain */
.ts-grain--animated::before {
  animation: ts-grain-drift 3s steps(12) infinite;


}
.ts-grain-flickered::before {
  animation: ts-grain-flickered 0.2s steps(4) infinite;


}
.ts-grain-flickered::before, .ts-grain--animated::before {
  filter: brightness(78%) contrast(100%);
  -webkit-filter: brightness(58%) contrast(600%);
  mix-blend-mode: overlay;
  z-index: 40;
}
@keyframes ts-zoom-in-out {
  0%   { transform: scale(1); }
  50%  { transform: scale(1.02); }
  100% { transform: scale(1); }
}
@keyframes ts-grain-drift {
  0%   { transform: translate(0,0); }
  25%  { transform: translate(-2%,1%); }
  50%  { transform: translate(1%,-2%); }
  75%  { transform: translate(-1%,2%); }
  100% { transform: translate(0,0); }
}
@keyframes ts-grain-flickered {
  0% { transform: translate(0,0) }
  25% { transform: translate(-5%, -5%) }
  50% { transform: translate(10%, 5%) }
  75% { transform: translate(-2%, 10%) }
  100% { transform: translate(5%, -2%) }
}

/* ─── §5c.1  PERFORMANT GRAIN PRESETS ──────────────────────────────────
   Lighter alternatives for when the original grain is too heavy.
   Uses fewer octaves (2 vs 4), larger tile size (600px), longer animation
   intervals, and fewer keyframe steps to reduce GPU/CPU load.
   Original classes above are unchanged — these are opt-in replacements.
   ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

/* Lighter grain SVG — 2 octaves, coarser, larger tile */
:root {
  --ts-grain-perf: url("data:image/svg+xml,<svg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' width='600' height='600'><filter id='gp'><feTurbulence type='fractalNoise' baseFrequency='0.5' numOctaves='2' stitchTiles='stitch'/><feColorMatrix type='saturate' values='0'/></filter><rect width='600' height='600' filter='url(%23gp)'/></svg>");
}

/* Performant grain base — swap to lighter SVG, larger background-size */
.ts-grain--perf::before {
  background-image: var(--ts-grain-perf);
  background-size: 300px 300px;
}

/* Performant animated grain — 6s cycle, 6 steps (1 step/sec vs 4 steps/sec) */
.ts-grain--animated-perf::before {
  background-image: var(--ts-grain-perf);
  background-size: 300px 300px;
  animation: ts-grain-drift-perf 6s steps(6) infinite;
}

/* Performant flicker grain — 0.5s cycle, 3 steps (6 steps/sec vs 20 steps/sec) */
.ts-grain--flickered-perf::before {
  background-image: var(--ts-grain-perf);
  background-size: 300px 300px;
  animation: ts-grain-flickered-perf 0.5s steps(3) infinite;
}

@keyframes ts-grain-drift-perf {
  0%   { transform: translate(0,0); }
  33%  { transform: translate(-1.5%,1%); }
  66%  { transform: translate(1%,-1.5%); }
  100% { transform: translate(0,0); }
}

@keyframes ts-grain-flickered-perf {
  0%   { transform: translate(0,0); }
  50%  { transform: translate(-3%, 3%); }
  100% { transform: translate(3%, -2%); }
}

html:has(.ts-grain){
	overflow: hidden!important;
}

.avia-slideshow.header.hero-section .avia-slideshow-slide {
	  animation: ts-zoom-in-out 52s infinite;

}


</style>
[/av_codeblock]

[/av_section]

[av_textblock fold_type='' fold_height='' fold_more='Read more' fold_less='Read less' fold_text_style='' fold_btn_align='' textblock_styling_align='' textblock_styling='' textblock_styling_gap='' textblock_styling_mobile='' size='' av-desktop-font-size='' av-medium-font-size='' av-small-font-size='' av-mini-font-size='' font_color='' color='' background='bg_color' background_color='' background_gradient_direction='vertical' background_gradient_color1='#000000' background_gradient_color2='#ffffff' background_gradient_color3='' padding='' padding_sync='true' av-desktop-padding='' av-desktop-padding_sync='true' av-medium-padding='' av-medium-padding_sync='true' av-small-padding='' av-small-padding_sync='true' av-mini-padding='' av-mini-padding_sync='true' fold_overlay_color='' fold_text_color='' fold_btn_color='theme-color' fold_btn_bg_color='' fold_btn_font_color='' size-btn-text='' av-desktop-font-size-btn-text='' av-medium-font-size-btn-text='' av-small-font-size-btn-text='' av-mini-font-size-btn-text='' container_tag='' fold_timer='' z_index_fold='' id='' custom_class='feature-list' template_class='' element_template='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-5fq3gg' sc_version='1.0' admin_preview_bg='']

 	<li>Pizza sats (from the first Bitcoin pizza transaction)</li>

Your Degent must meet <strong>ONE</strong> of these criteria:
<ul>
 	<li>File size of 400KB or larger</li>
 	<li>Inscribed on a high-value sat (uncommon or better)</li>
 	<li>Special artistic quality approved by the community</li>
</ul>
[/av_textblock]

[av_iconlist_item title='Wallet Setup' icon='play' font='svg_entypo-fontello' heading_tag='h3' heading_class='' link='' link_dynamic='' linktarget='' title_attr='' linkelement='' element_template='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-qh2ow' sc_version='1.0']
<ul>
	<li>Visit unisat.io and install the Unisat browser extension</li>
	<li>Create a new wallet or import an existing one</li>
	<li>Load your wallet with at least 0.001 BTC for transaction fees</li>
</ul>
[/av_iconlist_item]
[av_iconlist_item title='Prepare to Inscribe' icon='ue845' font='entypo-fontello' heading_tag='h3' heading_class='' link='' link_dynamic='' linktarget='' title_attr='' linkelement='' element_template='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-21ehc' sc_version='1.0']
<ul>
 	<li>In the Unisat extension, navigate to the "Inscribe" tab</li>
 	<li>Click on "Inscribe File" to begin the process</li>
 	<li>Make sure your Degent JPEG file is ready and meets all requirements</li>
</ul>
[/av_iconlist_item]
[av_iconlist_item title='Upload & Configure' icon='upload' font='svg_entypo-fontello' heading_tag='h3' heading_class='' link='' link_dynamic='' linktarget='' title_attr='' linkelement='' element_template='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-cxzyw' sc_version='1.0']
<ul>
	<li>Upload your JPEG file through the Unisat interface</li>
	<li>Set the fee rate (recommended: 1.1 sat/vb for reasonable confirmation time)</li>
	<li>If you're using a specific rare sat, configure those settings as well</li>
</ul>
[/av_iconlist_item]
[av_iconlist_item title='Submit & Confirm' icon='check' font='svg_entypo-fontello' heading_tag='h3' heading_class='' link='' link_dynamic='' linktarget='' title_attr='' linkelement='' element_template='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-97b7c' sc_version='1.0']
<ul>
	<li>Review all details and confirm the transaction</li>
	<li>Sign the transaction with your wallet</li>
	<li>Wait for blockchain confirmation (may take 10 minutes to several hours depending on network congestion)</li>
</ul>
[/av_iconlist_item]
[av_iconlist_item title='Submit to Collection' icon='megaphone' font='svg_entypo-fontello' heading_tag='h3' heading_class='fullwidth' link='manually,https://t.me/DecentralizedGentlemenClub' link_dynamic='' linktarget='_blank' title_attr='Submit to Telegram' linkelement='' element_template='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-mhwhxojw' sc_version='1.0']
Once your inscription is confirmed, submit your inscription ID to the <a href="https://t.me/DecentralizedGentlemenClub" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Telegram group</a> for inclusion in the official Decentralized Gentlemen Club collection.
<a class="btn mint-btn-primary" href="https://t.me/DecentralizedGentlemanClub" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><i class="fab fa-telegram"></i> Submit to Telegram</a>
[/av_iconlist_item]
[/av_iconlist]

<h4>Important Notes</h4>
<ul>
 	<li>Always double-check your transaction details before confirming</li>
 	<li>Keep your inscription ID safe - this is proof of your Degent ownership</li>
 	<li>Bitcoin network fees can vary; adjust according to your urgency</li>
 	<li>Community approval is required for inclusion in the official collection</li>
</ul>
[/av_textblock]

[av_icon_box icon='rocket' font='svg_entypo-fontello' title='Mint a Degent' position='left_content' icon_style='' boxed='av-no-box' font_color='' custom_title='' custom_content='' color='' custom_bg='' custom_font='' custom_border='' custom_title_size='28' av-desktop-font-size-title='' av-medium-font-size-title='' av-small-font-size-title='' av-mini-font-size-title='' custom_content_size='15' av-desktop-font-size='' av-medium-font-size='' av-small-font-size='' av-mini-font-size='' heading_tag='h3' heading_class='stat-header' link='' link_dynamic='' linktarget='' title_attr='' linkelement='' id='' custom_class='' template_class='' element_template='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-z3k5by' sc_version='1.0' admin_preview_bg='rgb(34, 34, 34)']
<p style="text-align: left;">Create a brand new Degent directly on Bitcoin. Be part of the original minting process.</p>

<ul>
 	<li style="text-align: left;">Fresh mint experience</li>
 	<li style="text-align: left;">Random rarity reveal</li>

[av_heading heading='Frequently Asked Questions' tag='h2' style='blockquote modern-quote' subheading_active='subheading_below' show_icon='' icon='ue800' font='entypo-fontello' size='35' av-desktop-font-size-title='' av-medium-font-size-title='' av-small-font-size-title='' av-mini-font-size-title='' subheading_size='16' av-desktop-font-size='' av-medium-font-size='' av-small-font-size='' av-mini-font-size='' icon_size='' av-desktop-font-size-1='' av-medium-font-size-1='' av-small-font-size-1='' av-mini-font-size-1='' color='' custom_font='' subheading_color='' seperator_color='' icon_color='' margin='' margin_sync='true' av-desktop-margin='' av-desktop-margin_sync='true' av-medium-margin='' av-medium-margin_sync='true' av-small-margin='' av-small-margin_sync='true' av-mini-margin='' av-mini-margin_sync='true' headline_padding=',,30px,' av-desktop-headline_padding='' av-desktop-headline_padding_sync='true' av-medium-headline_padding='' av-medium-headline_padding_sync='true' av-small-headline_padding='' av-small-headline_padding_sync='true' av-mini-headline_padding='' av-mini-headline_padding_sync='true' padding='0' av-desktop-padding='' av-medium-padding='' av-small-padding='' av-mini-padding='' icon_padding='10' av-desktop-icon_padding='' av-medium-icon_padding='' av-small-icon_padding='' av-mini-icon_padding='' link='' link_dynamic='' link_target='' title_attr='' id='' custom_class='hero-heading dash' template_class='' element_template='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-mlntn01r' sc_version='1.0' admin_preview_bg='']
Get answers to common questions about trust, self-custody, security, mining, and Plan B residencies.
[/av_heading]

[av_toggle_container faq_markup='faq_markup' initial='0' mode='accordion' sort='true' styling='av-elegant-toggle' colors='custom' font_color='' background_color='#10101069' border_color='#42424282' toggle_icon_color='#e228e2' colors_current='custom' font_color_current='' toggle_icon_color_current='' background_current='' background_color_current='' background_gradient_current_direction='vertical' background_gradient_current_color1='#000000' background_gradient_current_color2='#ffffff' background_gradient_current_color3='' hover_colors='' hover_font_color='' hover_background_color='' hover_toggle_icon_color='' size-toggle='15' av-desktop-font-size-toggle='' av-medium-font-size-toggle='' av-small-font-size-toggle='' av-mini-font-size-toggle='' size-content='' av-desktop-font-size-content='' av-medium-font-size-content='' av-small-font-size-content='' av-mini-font-size-content='' heading_tag='h4' heading_class='' alb_description='FAQ accordion' id='' custom_class='' template_class='' element_template='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-mlnwym4g' sc_version='1.0' admin_preview_bg='']
[av_toggle title='What exactly does Bitcoinnation help me do?' title_open='' tags='' title_pos='' slide_speed='' custom_id='' aria_collapsed='' aria_expanded='' element_template='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-i5e0u2' sc_version='1.0']
We help you take full control of your Bitcoin and your digital life. That includes everything from setting up a secure hardware wallet to running your own Bitcoin node to hardening your devices against cyber threats to securing a second residency abroad.
[/av_toggle]
[av_toggle title='Do I need to understand Bitcoin before working with you?' tags='Self-Custody' element_template='' title_open='' title_pos='' slide_speed='' custom_id='' aria_collapsed='' aria_expanded='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-fbq1kq' sc_version='1.0']
You do not need to be technical or experienced. Many of our clients are beginners who want to use Bitcoin the right way without guessing or searching forums. That said, a basic understanding of Bitcoin as decentralized, non-government money will make our sessions more productive.
[/av_toggle]
[av_toggle title='How does your pricing work?' tags='General' element_template='' title_open='' title_pos='' slide_speed='' custom_id='' aria_collapsed='' aria_expanded='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-etyolm' sc_version='1.0']
We like to keep our pricing clear and transparent. We charge fixed prices for our packages with no ongoing subscription fees. Our job isn’t complete until your devices are secured, and you know how to operate them properly.
[/av_toggle]
[av_toggle title='How do De-Googled phones improve privacy?' tags='Cybersecurity' element_template='' title_open='' title_pos='' slide_speed='' custom_id='' aria_collapsed='' aria_expanded='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-d09aa2' sc_version='1.0']
Most smartphones are tracking devices with a screen. Even when you’re not using them, they’re pinging your location, harvesting your data, and selling your attention. We help you switch to privacy-first phones that don’t include Google or Apple tracking.
[/av_toggle]
[av_toggle title='What kind of threats are you protecting me from?' tags='Cybersecurity' element_template='' title_open='' title_pos='' slide_speed='' custom_id='' aria_collapsed='' aria_expanded='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-antl9m' sc_version='1.0']
Most people think of cybersecurity as protection against hackers — and while that’s part of it, the real threats go much deeper. Your devices are constantly leaking data about where you are, what you do, who you talk to, and what you search for.
[/av_toggle]
[av_toggle title='Will my devices be harder to use?' tags='Cybersecurity' element_template='' title_open='' title_pos='' slide_speed='' custom_id='' aria_collapsed='' aria_expanded='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-8qxqkq' sc_version='1.0']
Not much about how you use or interact with your devices will change at all. You can go on living your online life without any unnecessary inconvenience. The only difference will be that your privacy and security will be far more robust.
[/av_toggle]
[av_toggle title='What is a Bitcoin Mining Calculator?' tags='Mining' element_template='' title_open='' title_pos='' slide_speed='' custom_id='' aria_collapsed='' aria_expanded='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-77fpka' sc_version='1.0']
A bitcoin mining calculator helps miners estimate their potential earnings. It analyzes key inputs like hashrate, power consumption, electricity cost, and the current BTC price to provide accurate profit projections.
[/av_toggle]
[av_toggle title='Why mine Bitcoin in Iowa?' tags='Mining' element_template='' title_open='' title_pos='' slide_speed='' custom_id='' aria_collapsed='' aria_expanded='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-6dpbm2' sc_version='1.0']
Iowa is one of the best places in North America for bitcoin mining hosting because of: Low cost power ($0.07-0.08/kWh), Low natural disaster risk, Clean renewable energy (solar & wind), and Cool temperatures that minimize downtime.
[/av_toggle]
[av_toggle title='What exactly is a Plan B Residency?' tags='Residency' element_template='' title_open='' title_pos='' slide_speed='' custom_id='' aria_collapsed='' aria_expanded='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-3ujn6i' sc_version='1.0']
A Plan B Residency gives you the legal right to live in another country, even if you’re not ready to move there yet. Think of it like geopolitical insurance. If things change where you live politically, economically, or legally, you’ve already got a legal escape hatch ready to go.
[/av_toggle]
[av_toggle title='Is this legal and above board?' title_open='' tags='Residency' title_pos='' slide_speed='' custom_id='' aria_collapsed='' aria_expanded='' element_template='' one_element_template='' av_uid='av-1zfnve' sc_version='1.0']
Yes. We help you arrange Plan-B Residencies in jurisdictions that don’t require you to move there to achieve full residency status. It acts almost like an insurance policy — it’s there when you need it.
[/av_toggle]
[/av_toggle_container]

[/av_one_half]
[/av_section]
